*** the session — keyed by the terminal

=subsystems/paladin/prototypes/ledger/sessions.js= — NEW, sibling of =Instances=. The key is what
the shell and every child share. UNMEASURED on a real pty (=tty.js= in the job tmp dir; run it in
your terminal).

#+BEGIN_SRC js
export class Sessions {
  constructor(paladin, dir) {
    this.paladin = paladin;
    this.dir = dir;
  }

  static async key() {
    if (!Deno.stdin.isTerminal()) return null;
    const { rdev, birthtime } = await Deno.stat("/dev/stdin");
    return { id: `tty-${rdev}`, opened: birthtime?.toISOString() ?? null };
  }

  async read() {
    const key = await Sessions.key();
    if (!key) return null;
    const file = this.dir.branch(`${key.id}.json`);
    const held = await this.paladin.read.json(file, null);
    if (held && held.opened !== key.opened) {
      await this.paladin.state.remove(file);
      return null;
    }
    return held;
  }

  async write(bag) {
    const key = await Sessions.key();
    if (!key) throw new Error("session selection needs a terminal — pass --ledger to write the machine default");
    const file = this.dir.branch(`${key.id}.json`);
    await this.paladin.state.json(file, { ...((await this.read()) ?? {}), ...bag, opened: key.opened });
  }
}
#+END_SRC

* release

- changed: ghost never authors `KEY=""`. An unset key is written `# KEY=""` by `ledger/init` and `instance/init` — it documents the key without claiming it, so a value set lower (machine-wide `~/.viva/.env`, the OS) shows through; the wizard fills the commented slot in place (`paladin.state.line` / `state.env`, `systems/ghost/belt/envfile.js`).
- added: a daemon enrolls an identity ONLY at `/userspace/handshake` (one upsert; `identity.enroll()`); every other route reads the user or answers `401 USER_NOT_FOUND — /userspace/handshake first`. kajuit handshakes before it attaches. (`subsystems/typology/gestalten/shard/secure.js authenticate/authorize`, `commons/lighthouses/multiplayer/provider/index.js`, `systems/runtime/daemon/aperture/userspace.js`)
- changed: `shard.secure.authorize()` no longer verifies+provisions in one breath — `authenticate()` (identity) sits at the daemon root; `INVALID_TOKEN` now only ever means the token.
- added: an environment key's `.default(fn)` mints its value once, at first `instance/init` — hello-world's `SECRET_VIVA_JWT` no longer prompts (`systems/ghost/belt/envfile.js fallback`)

earlier: no interface moved. (Rows 5, 6, 9, 10, 11 of the blast table will each produce one line here
when they land — all =fixed=; row 15 =added \`instances/untap\`=.)

* walk — docker ubuntu, shared tty — step 0 only (the tty-walk session)

beef: /"run the local readme.md installation steps step by step through docker container. tty open so i can attach besides you and you slop up the docs for both of us. run just step 0, notify me of issues, and tell me how to attach from my cli"/

** harness
- =docker run -dit --name readmen-tty --hostname readmen -w /root -p 127.0.0.1:12501:2501 -p 127.0.0.1:11794:1794 ubuntu:24.04 bash= — pid 1 is an interactive bash on =/dev/pts/0=; my step scripts run via =docker exec= and stream to =/proc/1/fd/1= (=tee /walk/stepN.log=), so =docker attach= / =docker logs -f= show them live. beef's shell: =docker exec -it readmen-tty bash= (=exit= there is harmless; =exit= inside =docker attach= kills pid 1 = the container).
- ALT host ports: at container creation beef's local instance held =127.0.0.1:1794= (=[::1]:2501= too) — publishing the README ports would have failed on 1794 and shadowed the local instance on 2501 (known-issues =readmen-container-shadows-the-local-instance=). Container-side ports stay 2501/1794; the 4-line =.env= edit at step 4 carries =localhost:12501= / =localhost:11794= as the PUBLIC remotes.
- the LOCAL tree again (=tar= of the working tree, 125 MB after excluding =commons/services/nlp/server/tmp= — a 649 MB stanza model mount that is gitignored, not repo content; the first tar was 811 MB). macOS =tar= wrote 59 AppleDouble =._*= entries (=commons/services/._nlp=, kajuit font licences) — none exist on the host disk; purged in the container; next tar sets =COPYFILE_DISABLE=1=.
- =~/.bashrc= carries =export PATH="$HOME/.deno/bin:$PATH"= (README line 3, persisted) and =export VIVA_PROCESS_ID=1= so beef's exec shell and my scripts share ONE terminal (pid 1).
